  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码审计工具已被纳入拉取请求审查流程，但其面对跨多个PR、提交或仓库历史分布的隐蔽变更时是否可靠尚不清楚。论文旨在区分不同变更组织方式对审计效果的影响，并检验“提供完整仓库历史”是否足以解决规避问题。

Method: 作者构建PRWeaver：从10个真实仓库中收集208个经执行验证的样本，并为每个样本生成4种匹配的审查呈现形式，共832个渲染实例。研究评估3类PR审计代理和6个审计模型系统，比较攻击拆分、按PR交错呈现、连贯载体融合，以及不同审查窗口大小和审查粒度下的检测率。

Result: 攻击仅通过提交边界拆分时，各系统检测率变化最多5个百分点，说明提交划分本身不是主要规避因素。相较之下，在每PR审查设置中，N=16的交错变更使检测率下降5至13个百分点；将目标变更融合进逻辑连贯的功能载体中，使检测率下降10至18个百分点。在N=24的全窗口审查下，检测率仅为16%至22%，而按PR审查时为50%至60%。

Conclusion: LLM代码审计的薄弱点不只是缺少仓库历史信息，而是其有限且受组织方式影响的活动审查上下文。当良性与目标变更共同占据上下文，或PR声明的功能目的能够合理解释相关差异时，模型更容易忽略关键风险。未来审计系统应强化跨PR因果关联、变更意图与代码行为的一致性验证，以及对上下文稀释和语义伪装的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-based code auditors are increasingly integrated into pull-request (PR) workflows, yet their reliability against adversarial changes distributed across repository evolution remains poorly understood. We introduce PRWeaver, a benchmark of 208 execution-validated attacks from ten real-world repositories, each instantiated under four matched review renderings (832 renderings in total). We evaluate three PR-auditing agents across six auditor-model systems. Across all systems, decomposing an attack changes detection by at most five percentage points, showing that commit boundaries alone do not explain evasion. In contrast, per-PR interleaving at $N=16$ and coherent carrier fusion reduce detection by 5-13 and 10-18 points, respectively. Under whole-window review at $N=24$, detection falls to 16-22%, compared with 50-60% under per-PR review. These results show that access to repository history is insufficient: concealment becomes most effective when benign and malicious changes jointly occupy the auditor's active review context or when the stated purpose plausibly accounts for the attack-bearing diff.

</details>


### [134] [Don't Regenerate, Debug: A Domain-Specific Agent for Repairing Near-Miss Hardware Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02712)
*Yansong Sun,Shenxiu Wu,Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Junhao Qiu,Junming Cao,Shudi Shao,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向GPU/NPU内核生成的自动调试代理，将大量“可编译、可运行但数值验证失败”的候选内核视为可修复资产，而非直接丢弃并重新生成。实验显示，该方法在成功率和单位成功成本上均显著优于多次重新生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM内核生成流水线通常通过生成、编译、执行和筛选大量候选实现来提高正确率，但多数失败候选被丢弃。其中许多属于接近正确的实现，已包含领域知识并消耗了推理、交叉编译与硬件执行资源。相比从零生成，调试的搜索空间更小、反馈更密集，因此具备更高的修复效率与成本优势。

Method: 作者构建领域专用调试代理，围绕三项问题设计：1）通过检索历史修复模式和加入诊断插桩缓解调试知识不足；2）采用反作弊检测与全覆盖评测保证修复结果真实有效；3）利用收敛保护机制和有界迭代限制控制调用与执行成本。该代理对数值验证失败但可执行的内核进行定位、修改、验证和迭代修复。

Result: Debug Pass@1达到66.7%，高于重新生成策略的平均Pass@1（25.9%）及Pass@3（40.7%）。相较于三次重新生成，调试方法每个成功内核消耗的token减少92.8%。消融结果表明，知识库是恢复能力的主要来源；完整性门控还会拒绝工作流初步接受结果中的12.5%至33.3%，说明仅依赖表面验证可能高估成功率。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将内核生成流程从“失败后重生成”转向“失败后可控调试”。该范式既能修复重复生成仍难以获得的近正确内核，也能显著降低交付一个正确内核的成本。其有效性依赖于可复用修复知识、严格完整性验证和受限迭代策略；摘要未说明基准规模、算子类型覆盖范围及跨硬件泛化情况，这些将决定方法的普适性。

Abstract: Kernel generation for hardware accelerators such as GPUs and NPUs has become a proving ground for large language models (LLMs), and state-of-the-art systems raise correctness through pipelines that couple LLMs with agentic reinforcement learning and evolutionary search. Such pipelines generate, compile, and execute large numbers of candidate kernels, discarding most of them and forgoing the opportunity to distill failures into reusable knowledge. Many discarded candidates are near-miss operators that compile and run but fail numerical validation; each embodies genuine domain knowledge and a nontrivial investment in LLM inference, cross-compilation, and hardware execution. We argue for a paradigm shift: rather than regenerate, debug. Debugging is far more constrained than generating from scratch: the search space is small and feedback is dense. We present a domain-specific debug agent that addresses three core challenges in autonomous repair: mitigating knowledge scarcity through retrieved patterns and diagnostic instrumentation, ensuring integrity through anti-cheat detection and full-coverage evaluation, and controlling cost via convergence guards and bounded iteration. Debugging serves two complementary roles: it extends the capability frontier by recovering operators that repeated regeneration fails to produce, and it lowers cost per deliverable operator. Debug Pass@1 achieves 66.7% versus Regenerate Avg Pass@1's 25.9% and Regenerate Pass@3's 40.7%, while consuming 92.8% fewer tokens per success than three-trial regeneration. Component ablations show that the knowledge base drives recovery, while integrity gates reject 12.5-33.3% of the successes the workflow itself accepted.

</details>


### [135] [HyperFL: Query-Adaptive Representation Learning for Software Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02967)
*Shuai Shao,Yiming Zeng,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HyperFL提出一种面向软件缺陷定位的查询自适应稠密检索框架：通过超网络为每个问题报告动态生成查询编码器的LoRA参数，在保持代码编码器固定且可复用的前提下，提升问题报告与源代码之间的匹配质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式缺陷定位方法通常以固定方式编码所有问题报告，但真实报告在文本长度、结构化程度和所含调试信息上差异显著。统一的查询表示难以同时适应不同类型报告，可能限制缺陷代码检索的准确性与泛化能力。

Method: HyperFL使用轻量级超网络，根据输入问题报告生成查询特定的LoRA低秩适配参数，并将其注入查询编码器，实现动态表示学习。代码编码器保持不变，因此可预先编码和索引代码库，避免每个查询都重新处理代码侧表示。该设计将适配能力集中于查询端，并可与多种嵌入模型骨干结合。

Result: 在真实软件问题定位基准上的实验显示，HyperFL在多个嵌入骨干模型上均稳定优于已有方法。相对当前方法SweRank，HyperFL在函数级定位任务中最高获得13.3%的MRR@10相对提升，以及16.7%的Hit@1相对提升。分析还表明，不同问题报告特征会触发差异化的适配模式。

Conclusion: 查询自适应表示能够有效缓解问题报告异质性对软件缺陷定位的影响。HyperFL通过超网络驱动的动态LoRA适配，在不牺牲代码侧索引复用效率的条件下显著改善检索排序表现，为高效、可扩展的自动调试与程序修复流程提供了有价值的表示学习方案。

Abstract: Software fault localization identifies the code locations responsible for reported issues and is a fundamental step toward automated debugging and program repair. Recent retrieval-based approaches formulate fault localization as a dense retrieval task by learning a shared embedding space between issue reports and source code. However, these methods encode all issue reports using a fixed query representation, despite the substantial diversity of real-world issue reports in length, structure, and debugging information. To address this limitation, we propose HyperFL, a query-adaptive representation learning framework for software fault localization. HyperFL employs a lightweight hypernetwork to generate query-specific LoRA parameters for the query encoder, enabling dynamic query adaptation while keeping the code encoder fixed and reusable. Experiments on a real-world issue localization benchmark demonstrate that HyperFL consistently improves retrieval performance across multiple embedding backbones, achieving up to 13.3% relative improvement in function-level MRR@10 and 16.7% relative improvement in Hit@1 over the state-of-the-art method SweRank. Further analysis shows that HyperFL learns distinct adaptation patterns for different issue characteristics, highlighting the effectiveness of query-adaptive representations for software issue localization.

</details>


### [136] [ConFL: Explainable Concurrent Fault Localization via Hierarchy-Guided LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02974)
*Shuai Shao,Dingbang Wang,Yiming Zeng,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConFL是一种面向并发缺陷的可解释故障定位框架，仅依赖缺陷报告并结合结构化并发知识提升定位效果，在八个大型Java项目上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据缺陷报告定位并发缺陷较为困难，原因包括信息不完整、程序实体提及具有误导性，以及跨线程交互复杂。现有基于大语言模型的方法还存在推理不稳定和可解释性不足的问题。

Method: ConFL从源代码构建并发知识库（CKB），利用大语言模型进行分层检索，将搜索范围逐步从组件级收缩到交互级并发上下文。同时设计交互级领域特定语言（DSL），显式编码共享资源上的跨线程交互，使模型能够聚焦关键并发关系，而无需遍历深层调用链。

Result: 在来自八个大型Java项目的真实并发缺陷数据集上，ConFL显著优于基于信息检索和基于大语言模型的先进基线，取得0.503的MRR和0.486的MAP。该方法对噪声缺陷报告、未见过的缺陷以及不同大语言模型骨干均表现出较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将结构化并发知识、分层检索和交互级DSL结合起来，可以提升基于大语言模型的并发故障定位准确性、稳定性与可解释性。

Abstract: Localizing concurrent bugs from bug reports alone is challenging due to incomplete information, misleading program-entity mentions, and complex cross-thread interactions, causing existing LLM-based approaches to suffer from unstable reasoning and limited explainability. We propose ConFL, an explainable concurrent fault localization framework that augments LLM reasoning with structured concurrency knowledge. ConFL constructs a Concurrent Knowledge Base (CKB) from source code and performs LLM-guided hierarchical retrieval to progressively narrow the search space from components to interaction-level concurrency contexts. An interaction-level DSL explicitly encodes cross-thread interactions over shared resources, enabling focused reasoning without traversing deep call chains. Experiments on real-world concurrent bugs from eight large-scale Java projects show that ConFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art IR-based and LLM-based baselines, achieving an MRR of 0.503 and a MAP of 0.486, while remaining robust to noisy bug reports, unseen bugs, and different LLM backbones.

</details>


### [137] [The Ground Is Shifting: A Reflection on the Foundations of Software Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03007)
*Thomas Bock,Audris Mockus,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文呼吁软件度量研究重新审视其长期依赖的软件开发痕迹数据假设，尤其是数据默认由人类专业开发者产生这一前提。在AI智能体参与版本控制等工具使用后，传统结论可能不再适用于当前数据，作者提出开展系统性的AI辅助复现实验以更新研究方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件度量曾依赖人工收集专有数据，随后借助版本控制系统及开源项目的痕迹数据实现了规模扩展。但既有实证研究通常隐含假设：这些痕迹来自专业人类开发者和真实软件工程活动。如今，工具使用方式、数据类型、数据规模及分析方法均已变化，特别是AI智能体可主动生成开发痕迹，使该假设受到挑战，从而可能削弱既有研究发现的有效性。

Method: 作者提出系统性的AI辅助复现计划：识别当代软件数据中基础假设被破坏的情形，选择软件开发、维护和演化领域的重要既有发现，使用现代数据、分析技术和AI辅助方法重新检验其可复现性，并据此研发能够适应新型数据生成机制的方法学。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果。其核心论断是，数据来源和分析环境的演变已经影响了部分既有软件工程实证结论；AI智能体生成的工具痕迹进一步使“数据由人类开发者产生”的传统前提失效，因此仅沿用历史度量方法存在有效性风险。

Conclusion: 软件度量研究需要立即检测现代数据中的假设违例，并通过系统复现和方法更新，建立在混合人类与AI活动痕迹条件下仍能产生稳定、一致结果的研究方法。这样才能维持软件度量在当代软件开发环境中的解释力与实际价值。

Abstract: For most of the past six decades, software measurement relied on labor-intensive manual collection of proprietary data, which hampered progress. The shift to repurposing traces from version control and related tools dramatically expanded data availability$\unicode{x2014}$especially with the rise of open-source software$\unicode{x2014}$but hinged on an often unstated assumption: that these tools are used by professional developers to build genuine software systems. However, as trace-generating tools, data types and scale, and empirical methods have all evolved, it has become clear that changes in data generation and analytical approaches affect many prior findings about software development, maintenance, and evolution. With AI agents now actively using these same tools, the resulting traces frequently violate the original assumption of human origin. To preserve the relevance of software measurement research, immediate action is needed: We must detect when foundational assumptions are violated in contemporary data and develop new methodologies that remain valid under changed circumstances. To this end, we propose a systematic AI-assisted replication program that revisits key findings using modern techniques, aiming for methods that yield consistent results on current data to keep software measurement meaningful.

</details>


### [138] [LLM Serving in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Frameworks, Methods, and System Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03036)
*Forough Majidi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对开源软件中大语言模型服务框架及服务技术的实际采用情况进行了大规模实证分析，发现vLLM在可见度与采用度上领先，开发者通常选择单一框架，但少量组合使用可覆盖服务栈中的互补能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署需要同时协调计算、显存、GPU资源、执行效率、延迟和吞吐量。尽管已有大量推理优化与服务框架研究，但这些技术在真实开源项目中的采用模式、组合方式及其与模型和系统特征的关系尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 研究识别并考察了五个面向大语言模型服务的框架：vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy和FlashInfer。分析维度包括框架单独及组合采用情况、服务方法类别、不同模型家族与模态下的采用差异，以及代码仓库的开发意图、应用重点、使用场景和架构设计。

Result: vLLM是最具流行度和采用度的框架。并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常使用的服务方法类别。多框架联合使用相对有限，表明开发者大多依赖单一框架；但采用多个框架时，通常用于连接服务栈中互补的能力。框架采用会随模型家族、模态、规模、领域专用性及部署环境而变化。仓库层面的案例显示，这些框架可支撑基于强化学习的推理、多模态生成与理解、微服务和云基础设施等应用与架构。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了开源生态中大语言模型服务框架采用实践的系统画像，说明框架选择具有明显的主导格局和场景依赖性。研究结果可帮助研究人员定位实践需求，帮助框架维护者完善互操作性和能力覆盖，也可为工程团队进行模型服务技术选型与架构设计提供依据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into software systems and AI services, making efficient LLM serving a concern for software engineering. Serving LLMs is challenging because inference requires computation, memory, GPU resources, and execution while maintaining latency and throughput. Although prior research has proposed LLM inference, optimization, and serving techniques and frameworks, little is known about how they are adopted in practice. In this study, we investigate the use of LLM serving frameworks and serving methods in open-source software systems. We identify and analyze five LLM-specific frameworks: vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, and FlashInfer. We examine how these frameworks and techniques are adopted individually and in combination, how adoption varies across categories of LLMs, and how repositories differ in intent, focus, use case, and architectural design. Our results show that vLLM is the most visible framework in popularity and adoption, while parallel computation, memory management, and network pruning are the most frequently used serving-method categories. Multi-framework usage is limited, suggesting that developers rely on a single serving framework; however, combined frameworks connect complementary capabilities across the serving stack. Framework adoption varies across model families, modalities, model sizes, domain specializations, and deployment settings. Repository-level analysis shows that LLM serving frameworks support applications and architectures, including Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based reasoning, multimodal generation and understanding, microservices, and cloud infrastructure. Overall, this study provides a large-scale empirical characterization of LLM serving framework adoption in practice and offers insights for researchers, framework maintainers, and practitioners working on LLM systems.

</details>


### [139] [Integration Barriers in Open-Source SSI Frameworks: An Exploratory Developer Experience Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03039)
*Breno Cerqueira Reis Nakamura,Arlindo Flavio da Conceição*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估开源自主管理身份工具的开发者体验，发现凭证接收等被动操作相对成熟，但模式定制等主动构建任务存在显著集成障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主管理身份虽具备去中心化数字身份的潜力，但其普及受制于集成复杂度高和工具链不成熟。研究旨在识别早期集成者在使用开源工具实现核心凭证生命周期任务时遇到的共性问题。

Method: 研究采用探索性探针研究，招募9名具备去中心化身份概念基础的开发者，使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask完成核心凭证生命周期任务。研究结合任务级难度评分与开放式开发者报告，并通过定性主题分析归纳障碍模式。

Result: 研究发现工具存在关键的抽象鸿沟：凭证接收等被动流程较为完善，而模式定制等主动构建流程暴露出较强的架构摩擦。主要原因包括API抽象不足、环境配置脆弱，以及文档无法及时反映生态系统快速演进。这些问题并非孤立可用性缺陷，而与现有框架的结构性设计选择相关。

Conclusion: 当前开源自主管理身份生态的主要挑战是结构性集成障碍。论文建议通过网页化沙盒、AI辅助模式生成器和可执行文档三类架构改进，降低开发者的配置与建模负担，并提升工具可发现性、可验证性和集成效率。

Abstract: Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) promises to decentralize digital identity, but widespread adoption remains hindered by integration complexity and tooling immaturity. This paper investigates the Developer Experience (DX) of open-source SSI tooling through an exploratory probe study. Nine developers with prior knowledge of decentralized identity concepts, representing early integrators building SSI applications, attempted core credential lifecycle tasks using Walt.id, Traction, and MetaMask. Our goal was to surface recurring integration barriers through qualitative thematic analysis of open-ended developer reports, complemented by task-level difficulty ratings. Our findings reveal a critical abstraction gap: while passive operations like credential receipt are relatively mature, active construction tasks, particularly schema customization, expose significant architectural friction. We identify that these barriers stem from inadequate API abstractions, brittle environment configurations, and documentation that fails to track the ecosystem's rapid evolution. These issues reflect structural design decisions in current frameworks. This study characterizes the structural integration barriers in the current SSI open-source ecosystem. To address the identified gaps, we propose three architectural shifts for developer tooling: web-based sandboxes, AI-assisted schema generators, and executable documentation strategies.

</details>


### [140] [Efficient Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Parser Stack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03065)
*Yongmin Li,Yihong Dong,Jia Li,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PSC，一种高效的语法约束解码方法。其核心是在预处理阶段将所有词表token的接受条件合并为针对解析器栈的单一分类器，使每个解码步只需检查一次解析器栈即可生成完整词表掩码，从而避免现有方法通常随词表大小线性增长的在线开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成代码、JSON等结构化输出时，需要语法约束解码来保证结果满足上下文无关文法。但现有GCD方法通常需要对大量词表token逐一判断是否合法，在线延迟会随词表规模增长，显著限制大词表模型的推理吞吐量。因此，论文旨在降低语法约束带来的实时掩码计算成本，使受约束解码的速度接近无约束解码。

Method: PSC在离线预处理阶段汇总词表中全部token对应的文法接受条件，并构建一个以解析器栈状态为输入的统一分类器。在线解码时，PSC不再逐token执行文法合法性检查，而是每一步仅检查一次当前解析器栈，通过该分类器直接得到完整词表掩码。该设计使在线掩码计算的理论时间复杂度不依赖词表大小。论文还分析了预处理成本在预处理服务提供方与解码用户之间的分摊方式，并给出用户自行预处理是否划算的盈亏平衡分析。

Result: 实验表明，在复杂编程语言文法上，PSC的掩码计算速度最高可比基线方法快700倍；在满足模式约束的JSON生成任务中，最高可快30倍。端到端结果显示，采用PSC后的大语言模型吞吐量可接近无约束解码的水平。

Conclusion: PSC通过将词表级合法性判断前移并合并到预处理阶段，消除了传统语法约束解码中与词表大小相关的主要在线瓶颈。该方法特别适合大词表模型和复杂结构化生成任务；其实际部署价值取决于预处理开销、文法与词表的复用频率，以及用户是否达到论文所述的预处理盈亏平衡点。

Abstract: LLMs are widely used to generate structured output like source code or JSON. Grammar-constrained decoding (GCD) can guarantee the syntactic validity of the generated output, by masking out tokens that violate rules specified by a context-free grammar. However, the online computational overhead of existing GCD methods, with latency typically scaling linearly with vocabulary size, limits the throughput of LLMs, especially for models with large vocabularies. To address this issue, we propose PSC, a novel grammar-constrained decoding method. By combining acceptance conditions of all vocabulary tokens into a single classifier of the parser stack during preprocessing, PSC can compute the complete vocabulary mask by checking the parser stack exactly once per decoding step, with time complexity independent of the vocabulary size. Experiments show that PSC computes masks up to 700$\times$ faster than baselines on complex programming language grammars, and up to 30$\times$ faster for schema-conformant JSON; end-to-end LLM throughput with PSC approaches that of unconstrained decoding. We analyze the preprocessing overhead for preprocessing providers and decoding users, and provide a break-even point analysis to help users decide whether to do preprocessing by themselves.

</details>


### [141] [Cross-Ecosystem Bug Classification in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03173)
*Mir Mohammad Yousuf,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对Qiskit及另外11个量子软件仓库共17,523个问题进行跨生态比较，发现经典缺陷占主导（约67%），量子特定缺陷占27%—30%。基于规则的可解释分类框架在细粒度量子缺陷识别上优于四种监督学习基线，并揭示不同生态系统的缺陷结构存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件由经典与量子组件共同构成，缺陷模式复杂且尚未被充分理解。系统刻画缺陷类型、严重性、质量属性及量子特定子类型，有助于为量子软件的测试、调试和质量保障提供可操作的依据。此前缺乏覆盖多个量子软件生态系统的系统性比较研究。

Method: 研究收集Qiskit的12,910个问题，以及包括Cirq和PyQuil在内的11个其他仓库的4,613个问题。作者采用基于规则的分类框架，从缺陷类型、类别、严重性、质量属性和量子特定子类型等维度进行标注与统计；通过统计检验评估框架在不同生态系统间的泛化能力；与四个监督式机器学习基线进行性能对比；并对2017—2025年的缺陷变化趋势进行纵向分析。

Result: 经典缺陷在所有生态系统中稳定占约67%，量子特定缺陷占约27%—30%。Qiskit中兼容性相关缺陷更多，其他生态系统则具有更高的语法类和量子特定缺陷比例。量子特定缺陷中，门操作与量子电路问题在两类生态系统中均最常见；非Qiskit项目的缺陷类型更具多样性，还包含算法、资源管理及经典—量子混合接口问题。统计结果表明，该框架在粗粒度缺陷类型层面具有泛化性，但在更细分类别上能识别显著生态差异。规则框架整体优于四个监督学习模型，尤其在细粒度量子特定子类型分类上优势明显。时间分析显示，量子特定缺陷比例总体稳定，并未持续上升。

Conclusion: 研究首次提供了量子软件跨生态系统缺陷分布的比较证据，说明量子软件的质量问题仍以经典缺陷为主，但量子特定缺陷具有明显的生态依赖性和细粒度多样性。可解释、可自动化的规则分类框架比纯数据驱动方法更适合当前的细粒度量子缺陷分析，可用于指导面向门、电路、接口兼容性及资源问题的针对性测试、调试和质量保障策略。

Abstract: Quantum software engineering faces unique challenges due to the interaction of classical and quantum components, which produce complex and often poorly understood bug patterns. Characterizing these bugs is essential for advancing testing, debugging, and quality assurance in quantum ecosystems. This paper presents a comparative study of 12,910 issues from Qiskit and 4,613 issues from 11 additional repositories, including Cirq and PyQuil. Using a rule-based classification framework, we analyze bugs by type, category, severity, quality attributes, and quantum-specific subtypes. Results show that classical bugs consistently dominate (67%) across ecosystems, while quantum-specific bugs account for 27-30%. Ecosystem-specific trends emerge: Qiskit repositories exhibit more compatibility related bugs, whereas other ecosystems show higher syntax and quantum-specific bug rates. Across both ecosystems, gate and circuit issues dominate quantum-specific bugs, though non-Qiskit projects reveal broader diversity, including algorithmic, resource, and hybrid-interface issues. Statistical validation confirms that the framework generalizes at the bug-type level while detecting significant variations at finer levels. Benchmarking against four supervised machine-learning baselines further shows that the rule-based framework consistently outperforms data-driven models, particularly for fine-grained quantum-specific subtypes, while longitudinal analysis (2017-2025) indicates that quantum- specific bugs remain relatively stable over time rather than exhibiting a steady increase. This study provides the first cross- ecosystem comparison of bug distributions in quantum software, demonstrating the utility of an interpretable, automation-ready, rule-based framework for guiding testing, debugging, and quality assurance.

</details>


### [142] [Fail-Fast, Restart-Smart: Early Failure Prediction and Restart for SWE Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03222)
*Chenyu Wang,Yunbo Lyu,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yaniv Harel,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出FailFast-RestartSmart：先用轻量监控器识别可能失败的SWE智能体轨迹并提前终止，再让同一策略在保留可选代码差异的条件下重新开始，从而同时降低推理成本并提升问题解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级软件工程任务的智能体轨迹较长，失败轨迹通常伴随重复探索、循环和更高的上下文成本。仅等待任务完成会浪费计算资源，但过早停止又可能中断潜在成功轨迹，或丢失失败过程中产生的有价值代码修改。因此，需要一种能早期识别失败、且能有效利用中间编辑结果的恢复机制。

Method: 方法包含两阶段控制器。FailFast是一个0.6B参数的轻量监控模型，基于终局标签和稠密的“失败转成功”监督，从可观测轨迹前缀预测失败概率，不依赖智能体策略的logits或隐藏状态。触发告警后，RestartSmart启动一个不带原始提示历史的同策略新轨迹，并将被中断轨迹的代码diff作为可选覆盖层；新智能体可自主查看、应用或丢弃该diff。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，仅使用Qwen3.6-27B轨迹训练的监控器可迁移至另外三种策略，其中包括闭源API模型。在5%目标误报率下，执行token节省14.6%至20.4%；对于Qwen3.6-27B，20.4%的节省优于逐步AgentStop改造版本的12.5%。在25%目标误报率下，RestartSmart将Qwen3.6-27B的解决率从66.6%提升至71.8%，而不继承中间diff的冷启动重试仅达到66.8%。

Conclusion: 早期失败检测可显著减少软件工程智能体的无效执行成本，而简单冷重启不足以改善成功率。将失败轨迹中的代码差异以可选择的方式交给后续同策略轨迹，能够保留中间工作成果并避免继承冗余上下文，实现成本节约与任务性能提升的兼顾。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents resolve repository-level issues through long trajectories that grow increasingly expensive as context accumulates. Failed runs tend to be longer and exhibit redundant exploration or looping, suggesting that some failures may be detectable before completion. Early termination, however, risks interrupting trajectories that would otherwise succeed; conversely, an unsuccessful trajectory may still contain useful repository edits. We present FailFast-RestartSmart, a two-stage controller for a single active trajectory. FailFast is a lightweight 0.6B monitor trained with terminal and dense fail-to-pass supervision to predict failure from observable prefixes without policy logits or hidden states. Upon an alarm, RestartSmart launches a fresh same-policy rollout without prior prompt history and offers the interrupted repository diff as an optional overlay that the agent may inspect, apply, or discard. On SWE-bench Verified, a monitor trained solely on Qwen3.6-27B trajectories transfers to three other policies, including a closed-API model, and saves 14.6%-20.4% of execution tokens at a target 5% false-positive rate; on Qwen3.6-27B, its 20.4% saving exceeds the 12.5% achieved by our per-step AgentStop adaptation. At a target 25% false-positive rate, RestartSmart raises Qwen3.6-27B resolution from 66.6% to 71.8%, whereas cold restart reaches only 66.8%. Together, these results support early termination with sequential same-policy recovery.

</details>


### [143] [NotDec: WebAssembly Decompilation With Inter-Procedural Type Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03286)
*Jikai Wang,Ningyu He,Tianming Liu,Junhai Wang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NotDec 是一套面向 WebAssembly 的反编译框架，通过 SSA 中间表示、跨过程类型恢复、内存 SSA 与结构化控制流重建，生成更可读且语义一致的 C 代码；其在重编译成功率和结构体成员访问类型恢复准确率上显著优于 Ghidra。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly 已广泛部署于浏览器、容器、物联网和无服务器环境。现有反编译工具虽然能够恢复部分代码逻辑，但通常存在输出冗长、可读性不足、复杂类型与数据结构恢复能力弱等问题，限制了对二进制模块行为的理解与审计。

Method: NotDec 扩展 WebAssembly 类型检查算法，将字节码提升为基于 SSA 的中间表示；采用 Retypd 进行跨过程类型恢复，并通过指针与数值的区分提高复杂数据结构推断质量；随后结合 Memory SSA 和保持语义的结构化控制流分析，输出结构更清晰、语义更一致的 C 源代码。作者还提供了更高效的过程内版本 NotDec_F，以缓解完整跨过程分析在大型二进制上的可扩展性问题。

Result: 在 5,241 个 Juliet 样本及全部 Howard 数据集程序上，NotDec 达到 100% 的重编译成功率，明显高于包括 Ghidra 在内的基线工具，其中 Ghidra 的成功率为 45.95%。在真实程序的结构体成员访问恢复任务中，NotDec 达到 85.33% 的准确率，而 Ghidra 为 9.24%。效率方面，NotDec_F 在未优化二进制上使用的内存不足 Ghidra 的一半，执行时间最多可减少 97%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 WebAssembly 的类型约束与 SSA、跨过程类型推断、内存依赖建模及结构化控制流恢复结合，能够显著改善反编译结果的可编译性、可读性与类型信息质量。其主要局限是完整跨过程版本在大型模块上的扩展性；过程内变体则在效率与恢复能力之间提供了更具实用性的折中。

Abstract: With WebAssembly widely supported in browsers, containers, IoT devices, and serverless platforms and increasingly adopted as a universal low-level bytecode standard, auditing its hidden vulnerabilities and malicious intentions has become critical. Decompiling existing WebAssembly modules can help security researchers and end users understand binary behavior, but current tools suffer from verbose result, poor readability, and limited type recovery.
  We present NotDec, an advanced WebAssembly decompilation framework. NotDec extends the WebAssembly type checking algorithm to lift bytecode into an SSA-based IR, applies the inter-procedural type recovery algorithm Retypd with pointer and numeric value differentiation methods to recover complex data structures, and leverages Memory SSA alongside semantics-preserving structured control-flow analysis to emit readable, semantically consistent C code.
  NotDec achieves 100% recompilation success rate on all 5,241 Juliet samples and all Howard dataset programs, significantly outperforming baselines including Ghidra (45.95% success rate). On type recovery accuracy, NotDec recovers 85.33% of struct member accesses in real-world programs, vastly exceeding Ghidra's 9.24%. While the full inter-procedural version faces scalability challenges on large binaries, the intra-procedural variant NotDec_F demonstrates superior efficiency, consuming less than half of Ghidra's memory and up to 97% less execution time on unoptimized binaries.

</details>


### [144] [Evaluating LLM Trade-offs for Enterprise Automation: Lessons from Workflow Generation in a Production Enterprise Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03311)
*Xavier Wrenn,Radoslav Raykov,Aleksandar Angelov,Hirokuni Kitahara,Yuji Watanabe,Anca Sailer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估六种大语言模型在企业IT自动化工作流生成中的表现。通过将单体生成改为分段式生成，工作流结构成功率从31.5%–82.8%显著提升至74.1%–97.8%，并使低成本小模型具备生产可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业合规与云工程场景需要在极短时间内根据运行时上下文生成或调整自动化流程，但传统静态编排器难以满足混合云、事件驱动评估及严格修复时限的要求。现有模型还普遍面临复杂JSON输出不稳定、成本和延迟难以平衡等问题。

Method: 研究在29个真实IT自动化场景中评估6个模型，对比单体式工作流生成与分段式生成两种流水线架构。每个“提示词—模型—流水线”组合独立运行8次，共完成2,784次实验。分段式流水线将生成任务拆分为变量脚手架、基础模块组装和嵌套模块生成，并测量JSON模式有效性、UI正确渲染等结构性指标，同时分析单工作流成本、交互延迟和模型选择权衡。

Result: 单体式流水线的结构成功率为31.5%–82.8%，复杂JSON生成是主要失败来源。分段式流水线将全部模型的结构成功率提升至74.1%–97.8%。其中，mistral-small达到95.7%的结构成功率，单工作流成本约0.01美元；mistral-medium-2505和gpt-oss-120b分别达到96.1%和97.8%的最高成功率。mistral-medium-2505相较mistral-small存在约19倍成本溢价。系统交互延迟可控制在50秒以内，单工作流成本范围为0.008–0.20美元。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于证明了分段式生成比依赖单一高性能模型更能稳定提升企业工作流生成的结构可靠性，并降低生产部署成本。论文强调结构有效性不等同于语义正确性：未来评估应进一步验证生成流程是否真正满足用户自动化意图、逻辑约束与执行结果。该方案为模型无关、可扩展的云端自动化平台提供了具有实践价值的设计路径。

Abstract: Enterprise compliance management requires rapid adaptation to evolving regulatory frameworks (e.g., DORA, AI RMF, FedRAMP) and tight remediation SLAs. Traditional static orchestrators often fail in hybrid cloud environments where event-driven assessments demand that automation code adapt to runtime context in seconds. This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [145] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对开源软件项目中的生成式人工智能治理政策进行大规模实证分析，发现多数政策旨在规范而非禁止AI辅助开发；强调透明度与责任的治理设计，较单纯限制性政策更能改善社区互动和代码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能正改变开源开发流程，但不同项目的AI贡献政策如何设计、政策是否影响开发者体验与项目质量，尚缺乏系统性证据。研究旨在填补这一空白，并为开源社区制定有效的AI治理规则提供依据。

Method: 研究分析了29,624个GitHub仓库，识别出385个已采用AI政策的项目。作者归纳出TRACE框架，涵盖透明度、责任、署名、约束和执行五个治理维度，并将政策划分为五类治理家族。效果评估采用倾向得分匹配和纵向双重差分方法，以估计政策采纳对维护者参与、AI披露、审查互动、代码质量及AI辅助贡献增长的影响。

Result: AI治理政策总体上以监管AI辅助贡献为主，而非全面禁止。政策采纳后，维护者参与度提高，AI使用披露增加，代码审查互动更丰富，代码质量得到改善；同时，AI辅助贡献仍持续增长。不同政策设计的效果存在差异：突出透明度和责任要求的政策，在社区参与和质量指标上的表现优于仅强调限制的政策。

Conclusion: 开源项目可以通过AI治理政策在维持AI辅助开发活力的同时提升协作质量与代码质量。有效政策不应仅依赖禁止或限制，而应优先建立透明披露、责任界定、适当署名和可执行的审查机制。TRACE框架可作为开源社区比较、设计和评估AI治理政策的参考。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [146] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蜕变关系（MR）的UI组件测试评估框架，用关系级指标衡量测试是否验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为，而不仅是执行代码覆盖率。结果表明，测试通常执行了比显式验证更多的行为关系，MR覆盖率可揭示传统覆盖率未反映的行为验证缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率只能说明代码是否被执行，难以判断测试是否验证了UI组件API与文档规定的行为关系。针对测试中存在的弱预言机问题，即行为被执行但缺乏明确断言，本文希望建立一种更贴近行为验证质量的评估方法。

Method: 给定组件源代码、文档和测试，利用面向UI的分类体系推断组件特定的蜕变关系，并将测试与这些关系进行匹配。框架结合确定性分析与语义分析，计算关系级MR覆盖率指标，包括MR Cover和MR Touch；同时通过人工评审验证MR空间及测试—MR对齐结果，并开展问题描述映射、预言机强化和与MR相关的注入故障评估。

Result: 在三种LLM配置下，MR Cover稳定处于42.5%至47.6%之间，并始终低于MR Touch，说明测试执行的行为关系明显多于其明确验证的关系。多数未覆盖关系属于弱预言机情形，即相关行为已被执行但缺少显式验证证据。MR覆盖率还发现了语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率无法单独揭示的行为缺口。大多数问题描述可映射到推断出的MR关系类型；弱预言机关系通常能暴露验证证据不足，MR标签在相关故障检测方面呈现积极趋势。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率能够从关系级别补充传统执行覆盖率，为评估现代UI组件测试中的行为验证程度提供更直接的视角。该方法尤其适合识别测试已触及但未充分断言的行为关系，从而支持测试预言机强化和测试质量改进。

Abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [147] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出RAV框架，通过任务感知提示路由、对齐式LoRA适配和基于公开测试执行的候选验证，在不修改基础大模型架构的前提下提升代码生成的功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成模型虽取得显著进步，但面对异构编程任务时，统一提示策略和单次生成通常难以保证功能正确性；同时，微调阶段与推理阶段的提示形式不一致也会削弱适配效果。

Method: RAV包含三个协同阶段：Route在生成前依据任务特征选择合适的提示策略；Align通过与推理提示对齐的LoRA微调减少训练—推理提示分布偏差；Verify生成多个候选代码，并通过执行候选代码在可见公开测试上的表现选择最终输出。实验在MBPP Sanitized和MBPP Full设置上进行，并通过组件消融、鲁棒性分析及数据污染分析验证效果。

Result: 完整RAV在MBPP Sanitized上达到0.8911，在MBPP Full上达到0.8520，均为所评估配置中的最佳结果；相较基础模型，分别提升6.35和9.92个百分点。消融实验表明，任务路由和对齐适配与执行验证结合时增益更明显。

Conclusion: RAV说明，代码生成的功能正确性可通过优化提示选择、参数高效适配和候选结果验证而获得显著提升，无需改变基础模型架构。其核心价值在于将生成前、生成中和生成后的优化环节进行模块化协同。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [148] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述自进化编码智能体，分析其定义、分类维度、软件工程特有驱动因素、主要挑战与未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体部署后通常保持静态，难以适应代码仓库演化、依赖变化、测试失败及历史修复经验积累。软件开发具有持续反馈和可执行验证机制，为智能体自我改进提供了天然场景，因此有必要系统梳理该新兴领域。

Method: 文章首先界定自进化编码智能体，并将其与传统编码智能体及一般自进化智能体区分开来；随后采用以进化对象为核心的分类体系，分析框架、记忆、技能、工具、模型和协作结构等变化对象，同时从进化时机和软件特定证据两个正交维度进行归纳。

Result: 综述发现，可执行反馈、仓库级上下文和编码轨迹是推动编码智能体自进化的关键软件工程要素。现有研究已探索多种自适应机制，但仍面临反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化能力等问题。

Conclusion: 自进化编码智能体是区别于静态编码智能体的重要研究方向。本文通过统一概念边界和分类框架，为构建更具适应性、可靠性和软件感知能力的智能体系统提供了研究基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [149] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于152名使用AI工具的软件从业者的问卷数据，发现AI辅助开发与团队“社区异味”（协作与沟通失调的社会技术反模式）之间并非简单的正负关系，而是取决于协作任务类型：在专业化工作中，AI通过促进同伴间知识共享间接减少异味；在协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，并补充而非替代人际互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具正在重塑软件团队的知识共享、协作与协调方式，但其对团队社会动态及协作失调问题的影响缺少实证证据。研究旨在澄清AI采用是否会增加或减少社区异味，并识别其中的作用机制与边界条件。

Method: 研究以交互记忆系统理论为基础，聚焦“专业化”和“协调”两个维度。作者验证了人机互动与人人互动的测量工具；依据既有文献构建社区异味变量，并通过专家调查和因子分析进行验证。随后，利用152份软件专业人员问卷数据，构建并检验5个偏最小二乘结构方程模型，以分析AI使用、知识共享、沟通质量、人际互动和社区异味之间的关系。

Result: 结果表明，AI的影响具有任务依赖性。在专业化导向的工作中，AI使用与更频繁或更高质量的同伴知识共享相关，而知识共享进一步与较少的社区异味相关，体现为间接改善效应。在协调导向的工作中，AI使用直接关联于更高的沟通质量；该作用并未取代团队成员之间的沟通，而是与人际互动形成互补。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发不应被视为必然改善或恶化团队社会协作的单一因素，其效果取决于团队正在进行的是知识专业化工作还是跨成员协调工作。研究贡献在于提供了经验证、基于交互记忆系统理论的解释模型和可复用测量工具。实践上，团队应针对任务类型设计AI使用方式：专业化任务中重视利用AI促进知识共享，协调任务中则应将AI作为提升沟通质量的辅助机制，同时保留必要的人际协作。

Abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [150] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CodeAssay：一个包含185个Python任务的软件工程代码生成与修复评测基准。其核心贡献是通过审计标准答案、公开与隐藏测试、变异测试验证及代码属性度量，提高大语言模型代码评测的可靠性与解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于测试的代码生成评测高度依赖参考实现与测试集质量，但错误标签、测试覆盖不足或测试泄漏都会影响结论；同时，仅以测试通过率衡量代码质量，无法反映复杂度等其他可观察属性。因此，作者旨在建立具有经过验证的真值、受保护测试和多维指标的评测框架。

Method: CodeAssay按分类体系构建185个Python任务，覆盖十类软件工程任务，并同时支持生成与修复评测。基准包含经过审计的参考答案、用于开发的公开测试、用于最终评分的隐藏测试，以及以变异得分验证测试套件有效性的机制。作者还选择若干代码属性指标，并对七个模型在不同提示配置下进行评测，比较正确率、统计显著性、程序长度、圈复杂度及静态分析发现等结果。

Result: 审计后重新判定既有模型输出，使1,890个正确性标签中的170个发生变化，占9.0%；总体正确率基本不变，但模型间最佳与最差表现的差距由11.9个百分点扩大至23.7个百分点。完整测试集和隐藏测试集的变异得分分别为82.6%和74.8%。七个模型的标准提示正确率范围为77.3%至98.9%，21组模型两两比较中有12组达到显著差异。在全部14个模型—提示配置均解决的120个任务上，没有任何模型在所有选定代码属性上均表现最佳。安全导向提示未显著改善正确率，也未稳定降低静态分析问题，却在所有模型中增加了代码长度和圈复杂度。

Conclusion: 论文表明，可信的模型代码能力评测不能仅依赖公开测试的通过率，而应结合经审计的参考真值、隐藏测试、测试套件质量验证和经过明确解释的多维代码指标。CodeAssay为AI辅助软件开发中的模型比较提供了更可复现、证据驱动的评测基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [151] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiHolmes，一个面向代码仓库级时间低效修复位置定位的LLM框架，并构建RepoEffi-Bench基准。该方法结合差分性能分析、紧凑执行路径提取和领域引导的LLM推理，在140个Python项目问题上优于多类现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间低效问题通常不影响功能正确性，因此不会产生测试失败或堆栈线索，传统故障定位和多数LLM方法难以处理。仓库级性能分析还面临单次运行噪声大、背景执行路径复杂，以及性能热点与实际修复位置之间存在语义鸿沟等挑战。

Method: EffiHolmes首先在默认负载与扩展负载下进行差分性能分析，以识别稳定的低效热点；随后提取连接热点与报告中低效函数的紧凑执行路径，过滤大规模背景执行；最后结合领域知识引导LLM推理，从热点和路径信息中定位导致时间低效的具体代码逻辑。同时，作者构建了RepoEffi-Bench，收录来自流行Python仓库的140个高质量问题。

Result: 在RepoEffi-Bench上的实验表明，EffiHolmes持续优于检索式、智能体式和性能分析式基线。使用GPT-5.1时，文件级Acc@3提升4.29个百分点；使用qwen3-4b时，函数级Acc@5提升15.00个百分点。该方法在不同模型能力下仍保持较好的稳健性。

Conclusion: 差分性能证据、路径压缩和领域引导推理的结合能够有效弥合运行时热点与真实修复位置之间的差距。EffiHolmes为缺乏测试失败和堆栈线索的仓库级时间低效定位提供了有效方案，RepoEffi-Bench也为后续研究提供了专门评测基础。

Abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable. Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [152] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ReBug：一个面向Web图形界面缺陷报告的上下文感知智能体系统。该系统能够从自然语言报告及已有工件中补全前置条件，在真实浏览器中执行复现步骤，并依据报告预期验证最终页面状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web GUI缺陷报告通常缺少复现所需的依赖、配置、测试数据或输入文件，使人工和自动化复现均较困难。现有方法主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏覆盖浏览器端完整交互、视觉执行和结果验证的端到端能力，因此需要一种能从报告中重建上下文并在浏览器中可靠执行的方案。

Method: ReBug将复现过程划分为两个阶段。准备阶段从缺陷报告和可用工件中重建缺失前置条件，并生成高层复现计划；执行阶段通过工具驱动真实浏览器完成页面交互，持续维护页面状态与历史操作的结构化摘要，并将最终状态与从报告推导出的预期结果进行比对验证。该设计强调显式上下文重建、状态感知执行和结果导向验证。

Result: 作者在4个开源Web应用的667份真实缺陷报告上评估ReBug。在受控的当前部署环境中，ReBug优于两个基线方法，平均复现成功率为49.96%，平均任务完成率为74.96%，平均动作执行成功率为86.54%。对历史缺陷版本的回放表明，成功生成的复现流程通常能够暴露原始版本中的缺陷行为。

Conclusion: ReBug证明了对于由自然语言缺陷报告驱动的Web GUI复现任务，补全缺失上下文、维护浏览器状态并进行显式终态验证是有效的关键机制。其主要贡献在于将前置条件重建、真实浏览器自动化操作和报告语义验证整合为端到端流程；但约50%的复现成功率也说明，复杂环境依赖、报告歧义和动态Web状态仍是后续改进的重要方向。

Abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [153] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并评测了多模态大语言模型在“截图转前端代码”中的视觉模式补全偏差：当重复界面中某个局部元素被刻意扰动时，模型倾向于输出与其他重复元素一致的代码值，而非忠实恢复截图中的异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页界面通常包含重复卡片、按钮和文本样式。此类规律虽有助于代码生成，但也可能诱导模型忽略局部视觉差异，生成模式一致却与截图不符的前端代码。现有截图到代码评测主要关注整体相似度，缺乏对这种局部视觉偏差的定量诊断。

Method: 作者基于Design2Code数据集筛选30个网页，构建首个视觉模式补全偏差基准。任务将重复UI模式中的一个局部元素扰动，并掩盖其HTML中的宽度或字号参数；模型需结合截图和HTML上下文恢复该参数。基准包含1,440张评测截图，覆盖卡片宽度等结构模式、文本字号等样式模式，以及标准和叠加噪声条件。实验评测5个前沿多模态大语言模型，并分析扰动显著性、元素位置、噪声及推理努力对偏差的影响。

Result: 所有被测模型均表现出明显的重复模式偏置。卡片宽度扰动的平均偏差率为69.78%，平均准确率仅21.17%；文本字号扰动的平均偏差率达到80.22%，平均准确率仅7.89%。Codex-5.3总体表现最佳，但其卡片任务准确率为68.61%，在文本任务中降至13.89%；Flash-3.0在文本任务中的偏差率达到96.11%。叠加噪声、更细微的扰动以及位于边界位置的异常元素，都会进一步提高偏差率。更多推理努力与较低偏差相关，但定性案例显示，模型即使能识别异常元素，仍可能最终选择与重复模式一致的错误答案。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多模态代码生成中的一类具体失效模式：模型会以重复视觉结构作为强先验，从而牺牲对局部异常细节的忠实性。偏差严重程度与异常的视觉显著性紧密相关。未来截图到代码系统应加强局部视觉核验、异常区域定位和基于像素证据的参数校准，而不应仅依赖整体模式一致性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [154] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ReCite，用于检测并修复Linux内核注释中因函数重构或删除而产生的陈旧函数引用。该方法在v6.18-rc1中发现869处问题；人工评估显示89.0%的修复建议有用，42.5%可直接应用，且提交的75个补丁中已有50个被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码注释中的函数引用可能不会随代码演化同步更新，导致引用目标已被重命名、重构或删除。这类由外部代码变化引发的注释过时会误导维护者并降低代码理解效率。已有代码—注释一致性研究主要关注Javadoc与其直接标注函数之间的语义不一致，无法有效处理内核注释中跨位置、历史演化导致的失效引用。

Method: ReCite采用三阶段流程：第一，识别注释中形似函数调用或函数名、但无法在当前代码库中解析到对应函数的“未解析函数形式符号”；第二，利用Git历史追踪每个未解析符号及其可能对应函数的演化过程，定位重命名、迁移、删除或语义变更线索；第三，结合演化历史与当前代码上下文，由大语言模型生成面向修复的建议，例如替换为现有函数、更新描述或删除无效引用。

Result: 在Linux kernel v6.18-rc1上，ReCite共检测到869个陈旧函数引用并生成相应修复建议。对随机抽取的200项建议进行人工评估后，178项（89.0%）被认为能够提供有用的修复指导，其中85项（42.5%）可直接应用。作者提交了75个修复补丁，已有50个被接受，说明该方法具备较好的实际维护价值。论文还对全部未解析函数形式符号进行了实证分析。

Conclusion: ReCite将注释失效引用问题建模为“当前无法解析的函数形式符号”与其历史演化之间的关联问题，并通过版本历史证据约束大语言模型生成修复建议。实验与补丁接受情况表明，该方法能够有效发现传统代码—注释一致性工具难以覆盖的陈旧引用，并为大型长期演化代码库的注释维护提供可落地的自动化支持。

Abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [155] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向终端用户界面（TUI）的自动化测试基准与工具链，评估多种前沿大语言模型及随机探索策略。核心发现是：随机测试在固定时间预算下因交互吞吐量更高而具有较强的崩溃发现能力，但LLM在单次交互效率和发现需输入触发的缺陷方面更有优势；自动推导启动输入的实际收益最大；代码覆盖率并不能可靠预测崩溃发现效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TUI兼具图形界面的有状态、屏幕导向特征与终端易部署性，已广泛用于开发者工具，但缺少专门的测试方法。对197个真实TUI应用的调查表明，只有12%的测试代码覆盖界面，而其中45%的界面测试不发送任何输入，仅检查静态画面，说明现有测试实践难以有效验证交互行为和运行时稳定性。

Method: 作者将真实TUI应用构建为无头测试基准，覆盖ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python和ink/TypeScript四个生态。每个应用被封装为带插桩的Docker镜像，记录可靠条件下的行覆盖率与组件覆盖率、渲染后的终端状态及崩溃信息。在相同墙钟时间预算下，比较四个前沿LLM驱动的测试策略与随机探索策略，并评估自动推导应用启动所需输入的影响。

Result: 没有任何LLM在所有指标上占优。随机探索是强有力的时间预算基线，其更高的崩溃发现数量主要来自更高的交互吞吐量，而非每次交互的决策质量。按单次交互衡量，LLM引导更有效，并且能够发现需要特定输入序列才能触发的缺陷。自动推导启动输入带来最大的工程实用性提升，使原本无法正常启动的应用进入可测试状态。行覆盖率与崩溃发现之间相关性不足，因此不宜单独作为测试有效性的代理指标。

Conclusion: 自动化TUI测试已具备可行性，但仍存在明显挑战。实践上，应优先解决启动配置与输入推导问题，并同时记录崩溃、交互效率、渲染状态和覆盖率等多维指标。研究评估中，诚实且吞吐量可比的随机基线比单纯比较模型选择更重要。论文同时发布了覆盖率工具tuicov和测试框架tuibot，以支持后续可复现实验与方法研究。

Abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates. Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Towards Automated Proof-Theoretic Semantics: Inference-Behaviour Semantics for 3-Dimensional K3 and LP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02654)
*Sophie Nagler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文将推理行为语义扩展到有限多维序列演算，并以三维序列系统K3与LP为例，比较其联结词意义及其与经典逻辑LK的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证明论语义方法需要能够系统地处理多值逻辑及不同逻辑之间的联结词意义关系。作者旨在推动推理行为语义与MUltlog框架结合，为任意多值逻辑的语义自动生成奠定基础。

Method: 作者基于序列演算的次结构性框架，构造适用于任意有限维序列演算的推理行为语义；随后分别为K3和LP的三维序列演算给出该语义，并将所得联结词意义与经典LK中的对应联结词进行比较。

Result: 分析表明，K3与LP中的联结词具有相同的意义；同时，这一意义是对经典LK对应联结词意义的保守扩展，即在经典适用范围内不改变LK联结词的既有语义。

Conclusion: 推理行为语义可有效推广至有限多维序列演算，并能统一刻画K3、LP与经典逻辑之间的联结词语义关系。该案例支持将其与MUltlog整合，以实现多值逻辑证明论语义构造的计算化与自动化。

Abstract: Inference-behaviour Semantics (I-bS) is a sequent calculus-based substructural approach to proof-theoretic semantics (P-tS), focussed on modelling the relationships of connective meanings across different logics. This paper provides a case study of how I-bS can be extended to any finitely multi-dimensional sequent calculus. To this end, we give I-bS for the 3-dimensional sequent calculi K3 (Strong Kleene) and LP (Logic of Paradox). We find that the K3 and LP connectives have the same meaning and that their meaning conservatively extends the meaning of the corresponding classical LK connectives. We situate this result within the wider project of integrating I-bS with MUltlog. The goal is to automate the generation of I-bS for arbitrary multi-valued logics using a MUltlog-style system, thus contributing towards the computational automation of P-tS approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Finite-valuation approximable structures: a solution to the Jung--Tix problem of probabilistic powerdomains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03073)
*Yuxu Chen,Hui Kou,Zhenchao Lyu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文构建了有限赋值可逼近域范畴FVA，并证明其具有笛卡尔闭性，且对次概率赋值与概率赋值幂域封闭，从而使赋值单子Vsub和Vone可限制在FVA上。该结果解决了广义Jung--Tix问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋值幂域是概率语义与域理论中的核心构造，但其是否能在具有良好计算性质的域类别中保持封闭，长期缺乏充分结论。论文旨在建立一个既足够广泛、又能稳定承载赋值单子的域范畴，以解决这一封闭性问题。

Method: 方法分两步：第一，对任意有限偏序集P，在Vsub(P)上构造递增的FS近似恒等族，证明Vsub(P)是可数基FS域。第二，将有限赋值可逼近域定义为：其恒等映射可逐点表示为一列递增映射的上确界，且这些映射均经由某些有限P_n的Vsub(P_n)分解。随后利用有限分离饱和定理与统一核提升定理，证明该性质在Scott连续收缩、有限积、函数空间及两类赋值幂域构造下保持不变。

Result: FVA是连续域的一个满子范畴，包含于带底元的可数基FS域范畴中；FVA对Scott连续收缩、有限积、函数空间、次概率赋值幂域Vsub以及概率赋值幂域Vone均封闭。因而Vsub与Vone可视为FVA上的单子。

Conclusion: 论文通过“有限赋值近似”这一结构性条件，为赋值幂域提供了稳定的笛卡尔闭语义环境。其核心贡献是证明概率与次概率赋值构造不会离开FVA，从而对广义Jung--Tix问题给出肯定答案，并为概率计算的域论模型奠定了更完整的范畴基础。

Abstract: We introduce the category \(\FVA\) of finite-valuation approximable domains, a full subcategory of continuous domains contained in the category of pointed countably based FS-domains. We prove that \(\FVA\) is Cartesian closed and closed under both the subprobability and probability valuation powerdomains. Hence the valuation monads \(\Vsub\) and \(\Vone\) restrict to \(\FVA\), yielding a positive answer to the generalized form of Jung--Tix problem, one of the longest-standing open problem in domain theory since 1990s. The proof is divided into two steps. First, for every finite poset \(P\), we construct an increasing FS approximate identity on \(\Vsub(P)\), and thereby show that \(\Vsub(P)\) is a countably based FS-domain. Second, we call a domain finite-valuation approximable when its identity is the pointwise supremum of an increasing sequence of maps factoring through spaces \(\Vsub(P_n)\), where each \(P_n\) is finite. A finite-separation saturation theorem and a unified kernel-lifting theorem then show that \(\FVA\) is closed under Scott-continuous retracts, finite products, function spaces, \(\Vsub\), and \(\Vone\).

</details>


### [158] [Right Divisibility in Erasing Semi-Thue Systems: A Minimal View of Intruder Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03274)
*Raja Oktovin O. P. Damanik,Alwen Tiu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究符号安全协议中的入侵者推导问题，从最小结构视角分析其可判定性：在所有函数符号均为一元时，问题可化为半Thue系统中的右可除性问题，并进一步考察更丰富的项重写系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般收敛理论下的推导问题通常不可判定，而现有可判定结果多源于具体密码学实例。本文旨在识别更基础、结构化的系统类别，明确哪些条件能够保持可判定性，以及从一元情形推广到更丰富项结构时会遇到哪些限制。

Method: 首先将一元函数项表示为词，把推导转化为判断是否存在词w使得wu等价于v的右可除性问题；随后分别研究若干半Thue系统，重点分析收敛前缀擦除系统和收敛后缀擦除系统。最后，将该视角推广至能够擦除上下文并提升特定子项或变量的项重写系统，并考察其推导复杂性。

Result: 论文在收敛前缀擦除系统和收敛后缀擦除系统上得到新的可判定性结果。与此同时，作者证明即使在收敛的同时变量提升系统中，推导问题也已经不可判定，说明更丰富的变量或子项操作会显著增加问题复杂性。

Conclusion: 右可除性方法为研究一元符号系统中的推导问题提供了有效且具有推广潜力的框架，但其向非一元、带变量提升的重写系统推广存在明确边界。收敛性本身不足以保证推导可判定，系统的结构限制仍是关键。

Abstract: The intruder deduction problem is central to symbolic security-protocol analysis: it asks whether an attacker can derive a target message from observed messages using (cryptographic) operators available to the attacker. Although convergent rewrite systems provide canonical normal forms, deduction modulo convergent theories remains undecidable in general, and existing decidable fragments are often shaped by practical cryptographic examples. In this paper, we study deduction from a minimal structural perspective. When all function symbols are unary, terms collapse to words and deduction becomes a right-divisibility problem for semi-Thue systems: given words $u$ and $v$ decide whether there exists $w$ such that $wu \equiv_S v$. We investigate this problem for several classes of semi-Thue systems and prove, to the best of our knowledge, new decidability results for convergent prefix-erasing and convergent suffix-erasing systems. We then extend this perspective to term rewriting systems whose rules erase contexts while lifting selected subterms or variables. Although these classes suggest possible decidable generalisations beyond the unary setting, we show that deduction is already undecidable for a convergent simultaneous variable-lifting system. This exposes both the potential and the limits of extending the right-divisibility results to richer equational theories.

</details>


### [159] [Statistical Verification of Quantitative Hyperproperties: Beyond Boolean Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03694)
*Amir M. Ahmadian,Hazem Torfah*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出定量超逻辑（QHL），以基于测度的量词和定量时间表达式扩展传统超性质形式化，并给出其统计验证算法与样本复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超性质形式化主要采用定性轨迹量词，难以表达现实系统中的概率、比例、分布等定量行为；同时，传统精确验证在复杂系统上往往不可行，因此需要可扩展的统计化验证替代方案。

Method: 作者将定性轨迹量词替换为基于测度的量词，并扩展时间谓词以支持更丰富的定量表达，形成QHL。针对QHL验证，论文采用统计验证框架：利用Hoeffding不等式估计测度量词相关概率并提供置信保证，结合极值理论处理嵌套测度量词；同时分析所需样本复杂度及可达到的统计保证。

Result: 论文构建了针对QHL规格的统计验证算法，特别覆盖嵌套测度量词场景，并从样本复杂度和置信度角度刻画算法性质。基于定量信息流控制基准的实验表明，QHL能够表达定性方法难以覆盖的定量需求，并在传统验证难以实施时提供有效替代。

Conclusion: QHL将超性质验证从纯定性关系约束推广到测度驱动的定量分析，使概率性、比例性和统计性系统属性能够被统一描述与验证。其统计验证方法在牺牲精确完备性的同时，提供了可控样本成本和明确置信保证，适合复杂现实系统中的定量信息流控制等应用。

Abstract: Formalisms for hyperproperties provide a solid foundation for studying the verification problem across classes of relational properties, such as those in information flow control (IFC). However, existing formalisms remain limited in expressiveness when it comes to capturing practical aspects of real-world systems. In particular, they do not adequately account for the quantitative nature of such systems. In this paper, we address this gap by revisiting the specification and verification of hyperproperties from a quantitative, measure-based, perspective. We introduce Quantitative Hyper-Logic (QHL), which replaces qualitative trace quantifiers with measure-based ones and extends temporal predicates with richer quantitative expressions. We further study the verification problem from a statistical verification point of view, and develop algorithms for the statistical verification of QHL specifications. For the introduced measure-based quantifiers, we particularly provide an analysis in terms of sample complexity and achievable statistical guarantees. In particular, we show how statistical methods such as Hoeffding's inequality and extreme value theory can be combined to develop statistical verification algorithms for nested measure-based quantifiers. Our approach provides quantitative alternatives for where traditional verification methods become infeasible. We demonstrate both expressiveness and efficacy on benchmarks from quantitative IFC, comparing against qualitative methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Micro-Segmentation Anomaly Detection in Zero-Trust Software-Defined Network Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02627)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究零信任软件定义网络中的微分段如何提升异常检测效果，并比较了ViT与1D-CNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粗粒度网络监控难以识别横向移动等细微异常行为，而零信任架构需要通过持续验证和严格分段降低隐式信任与扩散风险。

Method: 作者在模拟的零信任SDN流量数据集上，分别使用原始网络流数据和微分段后的流量数据训练ViT及1D-CNN模型，并以检测准确率和F1分数评估性能。

Result: 微分段输入显著优于未分段原始输入：分段数据训练的模型F1达到0.95，而原始数据模型F1为0.90。ViT整体略优于1D-CNN，尤其更擅长识别未经处理数据中不易发现的细粒度横向移动模式。

Conclusion: 将微分段纳入零信任网络能够有效增强入侵与异常检测能力。后续工作应在更大规模的真实网络数据集上验证，并研究可动态调整的在线分段机制。

Abstract: Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) principles need rigorous network segmentation and ongoing verification to reduce implicit trust and lateral threat propagation. This paper investigates anomaly detection in software-defined networking (SDN) systems by micro-segmentation, using deep learning models to detect harmful actions that evade traditional coarse-grained monitoring. Two models are developed: a Vision Transformer (ViT) and a 1D Convolutional Neural Network (1D-CNN), which are used to both raw and micro-segmented network flow data. Experimental findings from a simulated zero-trust SDN dataset indicate that micro-segmentation substantially improves detection accuracy. The models trained on segmented input demonstrate enhanced accuracy and F1-scores (F1 = 0.95) compared to those utilizing unsegmented raw data (F1 = 0.90). The ViT-based detector marginally surpasses the 1D-CNN, particularly in recognizing nuanced lateral movement patterns that are unnoticed in unprocessed data. These findings highlight the significance of including micro-segmentation inside zero-trust networks to enhance intrusion detection efficacy. Future efforts will broaden this methodology to include extensive real-world network datasets and dynamic online segmentation techniques.

</details>


### [161] [Secure AI Watermarking Framework for IP Protection in Multi-Tenant Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02656)
*M Anjan Kumar,Kishor Kumar Gajula,Ch Prathima*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 摘要提出一种面向云端多租户、联邦学习与远程学习场景的安全AI水印框架，结合多方密钥分发、认证、访问限制、主动预警及事后追踪机制，以降低数据、模型和设备IP泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云端AI服务、多租户部署及联邦学习的发展，数据和模型在跨设备、跨主体交换时可能发生未授权访问、泄露和所有权争议。作者试图通过水印、身份认证和密钥管理增强AI资产的保护与可追溯性。

Method: 方法描述包括：在私有认证平台中部署安全水印系统；将密钥分发给可信参与方并基于认证机制进行验证；通过域级访问限制实施预防性控制；使用主动安全告警机制在攻击发生前进行防护；在泄露发生后，借助水印和生物识别信息追踪特定设备及相关IP泄露。

Result: 摘要未提供实验设计、数据集、威胁模型、算法细节、性能指标或与基线方法的比较，因此无法验证所提系统在水印鲁棒性、泄露检测率、误报率、计算开销、密钥安全性和联邦学习适配性方面的实际效果。

Conclusion: 该工作关注的安全问题具有现实意义，并提出将预防、检测、追踪和访问控制整合到AI水印保护框架中的思路。但摘要表述不够清晰，术语和系统边界较模糊；论文需要进一步明确系统架构、密钥分发协议、水印嵌入与验证算法、生物识别的安全与隐私处理、攻击模型，以及可复现的实验评估，才能证明方案的有效性。

Abstract: The Secured data safe guard transaction with multi-tenant environments run on private-protected authenticate platforms runs by secured handed environments that emerges with the expansion of cloud-based AI services. To enhanced this secured leakage address challenges solution to protect a secure AI Watermarking system incorporating key distributed between trusted parties based on key authentication as we proposed solution to guided safe guarded way to reactive, and proactive security alert systems using algorithms. This proposed system before attacks can be prevented through the active measures. domain run base restrictions with limited access. Conversely, Proposed system reactive methods to captured on watermarking and biometric identification owner device specific IP leakage that occur during the exchange of data and models in federated and remote learning algorithms.

</details>


### [162] [Your Agentic LLMs Secretly Encode Latent Signals of Indirect Prompt-Injection Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02657)
*Jianshuo Dong,Yiming Liu,Maosen Zhang,Nan Deng,Xu Peng,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“间接提示注入暴露（IPI exposure）”概念，并表明可通过生成前隐藏状态上的线性探针高精度检测智能体大模型是否受到恶意外部工具结果影响；基于该检测信号，作者构建AGRI防御机制以降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注如何防御间接提示注入，但对智能体大模型在遭受此类注入时内部表征发生了什么、模型是否已隐式识别风险却未采取安全行动，缺少系统性理解。论文旨在刻画这种内部风险信号，并将其转化为有效防御。

Method: 研究包含三部分：第一，在六个模型上使用生成前隐藏状态训练简单线性探针，评估其对IPI暴露的预测能力，并测试其对未见攻击、不同智能体指令、任务集合、自适应攻击及跨语言场景的泛化性。第二，通过思维链测量分析模型的“识别—行动鸿沟”，随后提出AGRI：当探针检测到风险时，动态前置抗注入推理步骤，再由模型完成任务。第三，提出解释分析框架，寻找与探针信号相关性最高的自然语言解释，以比较不同模型内部信号的语义特征。

Result: 线性探针能够在未见攻击、指令和任务套件上达到90%以上AUROC，并在自适应及跨语言条件下保持较强鲁棒性。思维链分析显示，模型常能编码或识别注入风险，但并不总能据此执行安全行为。AGRI在较难的AgentDojo评测中显著降低攻击成功率；例如，对Qwen3.5-27B，攻击成功率从34.6%降至0%，同时基本维持正常任务效用。解释结果表明，不同模型的潜在信号并不一致：有些对应直接的IPI暴露判断，有些则更接近间接的操作性风险线索。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将间接提示注入风险从外部行为评测推进到内部表征检测：大模型隐藏状态中存在可泛化、可线性读取的IPI暴露信号。该信号可作为按需触发推理式防御的门控条件，从而缓解模型“知道风险但未安全行动”的问题。其局限性可能在于，结论依赖特定模型、评测环境和探针标注方式；AGRI的额外推理成本、面对更强分布外攻击时的稳定性，以及探针误报和漏报对实际部署的影响，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI) attacks, e.g., malicious side-tasks hidden in external tool results. While many efforts have sought to address the threats, little is known about the internals of agentic LLMs when they are exposed to IPI attacks, a condition which we call IPI exposure. In this paper, we study this problem in depth from three aspects. (1) Probing: Across six models, including the giant 753B-parameter GLM-5.2, simple linear probes trained on pre-generation hidden states can predict LLMs' IPI exposure. These probes achieve 90%+ AUROC on unseen attacks, agent instructions, and task suites; they exhibit high robustness under adaptive attacks and in cross-lingual settings. (2) Defense: Our CoT measurement reveals a recognition--action gap: though models encode such signals, they often fail to translate them into safe actions. We then introduce AGRI, a probe-gated reasoning-based defense that prepends anti-injection reasoning on demand. On difficult AgentDojo settings, AGRI substantially reduces attack success rate, e.g., from 34.6% to 0% on Qwen3.5-27B, while largely maintaining clean-task utility. (3) Explanation: We introduce an analysis framework that identifies natural-language explanations most strongly correlated with probe-captured signals. The resulting profiles differ across models: latent signals can align with either direct IPI-exposure claims or indirect operational cues. Code is available: https://github.com/jianshuod/IPI-exposure-signal.

</details>


### [163] [ZK-SR117: A Chunked Zero-Knowledge Attestation Design for Aggregated Fair-Lending Metrics, with a Control Mapping toward Full SR 11-7 Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02664)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Asaduzzaman Anik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种分块式零知识证明（zkSNARK）架构，用于在不暴露模型参数或客户数据的前提下，对机器学习模型的公平性与校准性进行可审计证明。其在32,768条已承诺数据上完成端到端验证，并显示该分块方案较平坦求和和树形归约电路更具可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管场景中的模型部署需要向审计方证明模型控制措施有效，但直接共享训练数据、客户记录或模型权重会带来隐私与商业机密风险。论文旨在将监管性模型风险管理要求转化为可验证的零知识声明，从而兼顾审计可信度、数据保密性与抗选择性抽样能力。

Method: 作者设计了分块零知识电路：将已承诺、经随机数采样的数据批次拆分为多个块，分别生成和验证证明，再聚合统计结果。已实现两类控制指标：人口统计均等差距（demographic-parity gap）和10个分箱下的预期校准误差（ECE）。实验将该架构与平坦求和电路及树形归约电路比较；同时提出基于随机数的采样协议、部署威胁模型，以及将监管控制语言映射为九类零知识证明控制元素的设计框架。

Result: 在32,768行数据和32个独立证明的实验中，公平性指标的证明结果与明文留出集真实值相差不超过0.0029，每块证明时间不足4秒。对于ECE，32个块均成功验证，单块证明约14.7秒，证明值与明文结果的差异不超过0.00037。平坦求和电路在数千行后发生溢出，树形归约电路虽数值精确但编译成本难以承受；分块设计是唯一达到该规模的方案。作者还发现并修复了会同时扭曲证明过程与公平性统计量的哨兵编码异常值问题，并发布了预处理规范。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了分块零知识证明可用于隐私保护型模型审计，并可在较大规模数据上验证至少两项关键模型控制指标。其主要贡献在于工程可扩展性、可复用的控制验证架构及数据质量治理发现。不过，端到端实验仅覆盖一个模型类别、一个任务和两项控制；更完整的监管控制映射、采样协议和威胁模型仍属设计方案，尚需在更多模型、指标、数据规模及真实审计流程中验证。

Abstract: Deploying ML models in regulated decision-making (credit underwriting, fraud detection, loan approval) requires demonstrating fairness and robustness to auditors without exposing model weights or customer data. We address this attestation problem for U.S. bank supervision under SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 guidance. We present a chunked zero-knowledge circuit design that attests an aggregated fairness statistic - the demographic-parity gap - on committed, nonce-sampled batches of real 2022 HMDA mortgage data, and demonstrate it end-to-end: 32,768 rows, 32 independently verified zkSNARK proofs, aggregated attested gap within 0.0029 of the true held-out value, per-chunk proving under 4 seconds. We also demonstrate extensibility by attesting a second control on the identical architecture - expected calibration error at 10 bins - with all 32 chunks verified, per-chunk proving at about 14.7 seconds, and attested ECE within 0.00037 of the plaintext value on the same committed rows. We compare this design against two alternatives - a flat summation circuit, which overflows past a few thousand rows, and a tree-reduction circuit, numerically exact but intractable to compile - and find the chunked design is the only one that reached this scale. We discovered and root-caused a genuine data-quality failure (a sentinel-code outlier distorting circuit proving and the fairness statistic itself) and resolved it with a published preprocessing specification. We also propose a fuller mapping from SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 control language to zero-knowledge statements (nine control elements spanning soundness, calibration, robustness, and drift), a nonce-based sampling protocol resisting bank-side cherry-picking, and a threat model for deployment, proposed as design work, not implemented results. Two controls, one model class, one task are demonstrated end-to-end; the rest is scoped and left as future work.

</details>


### [164] [Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02665)
*Yongxi Zhou,Junwei Yao,Yuanzhe Liu,Zihan Dong,Wenbo Ye,Jiaxi Wen,Lai Yun Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，仅评估基准题目的单一规范表述会系统性低估模型在安全任务中的不安全行为；对同一意图进行多种语义保持的改写后，跨表述取并集能发现更多风险暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数应能代表模型对题目意图的行为，但单一表面形式可能无法忠实估计模型表现。研究旨在区分表述变化带来的真实行为差异与解码或评审噪声，并量化安全评测中的漏检程度。

Method: 研究使用370个种子题目、5种预先授权的表述形式和5个模型，采用机器回译与矩阵语言框架代码切换生成改写，避免依赖大语言模型及后验操控。所有回答由同一经过人工校准、跨供应商的评审器评分，并验证意图保持；通过McNemar检验、bootstrap置信区间、重复采样及XSTest良性控制评估表述效应、随机性和双向不稳定性。

Result: 没有一种改写形式始终最容易诱发不安全行为；20项逐变换比较中仅6项经多重校正后显著，且多数表现为保护作用。相比之下，多表述结果的风险并集比最危险的单一表述高3.3至12.9个百分点，5个模型的bootstrap 95%置信区间均排除零；约5%至13%的题目在规范表述下表现安全，却在某种改写下出现不安全行为。规范表述重复采样未发现新的暴露，说明该差异并非单纯随机解码噪声；模型间差距明显，Gemini 2.5 Pro最大。单一表述平均仅覆盖约53%的观测风险，约三种表述可达到85%覆盖。

Conclusion: 安全基准若只使用单一规范表述，可能低估模型风险并产生不稳健结论。多表述评估更适合刻画表面形式敏感性和潜在风险，但当前覆盖率结论仅适用于所选表述集合，不能外推至所有可能改写。研究发布了数据集、代码和逐回答标签以支持复现。

Abstract: A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.

</details>


### [165] [Vulnerabilities, Secrets and Misconfiguration in the Highest-Exposure Docker Hub Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02669)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Beatriz Machado,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ChimangoScan，对Docker Hub进行生态级容器镜像安全测量。研究发现：高暴露镜像中已知漏洞、严重漏洞与CIS配置错误几乎普遍存在；不同扫描器之间的一致性极低，单一工具会显著扭曲安全态势判断；秘密检测器的告警绝大多数为误报或非凭据内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Docker Hub生态测量通常依赖单一安全检测工具，因而无法量化工具选择对漏洞统计结果的影响。已有多扫描器对比研究又仅覆盖几十至数百个镜像，缺乏生态级证据。论文旨在同时评估镜像供应链中的漏洞传播范围、镜像暴露度与安全风险的关系，以及多类扫描工具的检测差异。

Method: 作者构建ChimangoScan流水线：爬取12,716,568个Docker Hub仓库及其累计拉取数据，重建包含5,440万个IS_BASE_OF关系的镜像层依赖图；定义暴露度分数，将镜像自身拉取量与整个下游子树拉取量汇总；选择暴露度最高的52,895个仓库进行扫描，其覆盖全体记录拉取量的84.7%。研究使用6个独立扫描器，并对漏洞扫描器、配置检查器和秘密检测器的结果进行比较；此外对1,100条随机秘密检测结果进行人工标注。

Result: 扫描共获得1.704亿条发现。96.3%的镜像包含已知软件包漏洞，93.4%包含严重漏洞，98.0%至少存在一项CIS Docker Benchmark配置错误。对于8,070万个不同的“漏洞-软件包”组合，仅66.8%由某一个漏洞扫描器独自发现，只有2.7%被三个漏洞扫描器共同发现；性能最好的单一扫描器也只能覆盖66.9%的组合。TruffleHog在76.9%的镜像中报告秘密，但人工标注表明其中99.7%并非真实凭据。一个zlib漏洞影响的镜像暴露度占整个语料总暴露度的47.3%，并传播至113万个不同下游镜像。镜像暴露度与其漏洞程度之间不存在有效预测关系。

Conclusion: Docker Hub中高影响力镜像的安全问题高度普遍，且基础镜像依赖会放大漏洞传播。生态级风险测量不能依赖单一扫描器，因为扫描器差异会主导统计结论；秘密扫描结果尤其需要严格验证，不能将告警数直接视为真实凭据泄露数。暴露度可用于识别应优先治理的供应链节点，但不能替代对镜像实际漏洞状态的评估。论文发布了流水线及283GB数据集，为后续可复现的容器供应链安全研究提供基础。

Abstract: Docker Hub is the registry underneath most container deployments, and a flaw in a widely reused base image is inherited by every image built on it. Prior ecosystem-scale measurements each rely on a single detector, leaving the tool-dependence of their counts unquantified, while the studies that do compare scanners use samples of tens to hundreds of images.
